Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a membranes algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 738. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 231/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to membranes theory, we decompose the problem into 738 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 231. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
